```
        analysis_prediction_space: str = "normalized",
        training_context_space: str = "normalized",
        **kwargs,
    ):
        super().__init__(**kwargs)
        self.inference_rollout_state_space = inference_rollout_state_space.lower()
        self.inference_cost_space = inference_cost_space.lower()
        self.inference_cost_type = inference_cost_type.lower()
        self.analysis_prediction_space = analysis_prediction_space.lower()
        self.training_context_space = training_context_space.lower()

        valid_spaces = {"raw", "normalized", "sphere"}
        if self.inference_rollout_state_space not in valid_spaces:
            raise ValueError(
                "Unsupported inference_rollout_state_space: "
                f"{self.inference_rollout_state_space}"
            )
        if self.inference_cost_space not in valid_spaces:
            raise ValueError(f"Unsupported inference_cost_space: {self.inference_cost_space}")
        if self.inference_cost_type not in {"cosine", "mse"}:
            raise ValueError(f"Unsupported inference_cost_type: {self.inference_cost_type}")
        if self.analysis_prediction_space not in valid_spaces:
            raise ValueError(
                f"Unsupported analysis_prediction_space: {self.analysis_prediction_space}"
            )
        if self.training_context_space not in valid_spaces:
            raise ValueError(f"Unsupported training_context_space: {self.training_context_space}")

    def normalize_embeddings(self, emb):
        return F.normalize(emb, dim=-1, eps=1e-8)

    def _get_rollout_state_space(self) -> str:
        return getattr(self, "inference_rollout_state_space", "normalized").lower()

    def _get_cost_space(self) -> str:
        return getattr(self, "inference_cost_space", "normalized").lower()

    def _get_cost_type(self) -> str:
        return getattr(self, "inference_cost_type", "cosine").lower()

    def _resolve_space_name(self, space: str) -> str:
        if space == "sphere":
            return "normalized"
        return space

    def _select_embedding_space(self, *, emb_raw, emb_norm, space: str):
        space = self._resolve_space_name(space.lower())
        if space == "raw":
            return emb_raw
        if space == "normalized":
            return emb_norm
        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported embedding space: {space}")

    def encode(self, info):
        info = super().encode(info)
        info["emb_raw"] = info["emb"]
        info["emb"] = self.normalize_embeddings(info["emb_raw"])
        return info

    def predict_raw(self, emb, act_emb):
        return super().predict(emb, act_emb)

    def predict(self, emb, act_emb):
        preds = self.predict_raw(emb, act_emb)
        return self.normalize_embeddings(preds)

    def rollout(self, info, action_sequence, history_size: int = 3):
        """Rollout while keeping raw and normalized trajectories side by side.

        `inference_rollout_state_space` decides what the predictor consumes
        autoregressively:
        - `normalized`: original spherical branch
        - `raw`: feed back raw predictor outputs directly
        """

        assert "pixels" in info, "pixels not in info_dict"
        H = info["pixels"].size(2)
        B, S, T = action_sequence.shape[:3]
        act_0, act_future = torch.split(action_sequence, [H, T - H], dim=2)
        info["action"] = act_0
        n_steps = T - H

        _init = {k: v[:, 0] for k, v in info.items() if torch.is_tensor(v)}
        _init = self.encode(_init)
        init_raw = _init["emb_raw"].unsqueeze(1).expand(B, S, -1, -1)
        init_norm = _init["emb"].unsqueeze(1).expand(B, S, -1, -1)

        rollout_raw = rearrange(init_raw, "b s ... -> (b s) ...").clone()
        rollout_norm = rearrange(init_norm, "b s ... -> (b s) ...").clone()
        rollout_state = self._select_embedding_space(
            emb_raw=rollout_raw,
            emb_norm=rollout_norm,
            space=self._get_rollout_state_space(),
        ).clone()

        act_full = rearrange(action_sequence, "b s ... -> (b s) ...")
        act_emb_full = self.action_encoder(act_full)

        HS = history_size
        for step in range(n_steps + 1):
            action_end = H + step
            # Match the original "take up to the last HS steps" behavior even
            # when the available prefix is shorter than HS.
            window = min(HS, action_end)
            emb_trunc = rollout_state[:, -window:]
            act_trunc = act_emb_full[:, action_end - window : action_end]
            pred_raw = self.predict_raw(emb_trunc, act_trunc)[:, -1:]
            pred_norm = self.normalize_embeddings(pred_raw)
            pred_state = self._select_embedding_space(
                emb_raw=pred_raw,
                emb_norm=pred_norm,
                space=self._get_rollout_state_space(),
            )

            rollout_raw = torch.cat([rollout_raw, pred_raw], dim=1)
            rollout_norm = torch.cat([rollout_norm, pred_norm], dim=1)
            rollout_state = torch.cat([rollout_state, pred_state], dim=1)

        info["predicted_emb_raw"] = rearrange(rollout_raw, "(b s) ... -> b s ...", b=B, s=S)
        info["predicted_emb"] = rearrange(rollout_norm, "(b s) ... -> b s ...", b=B, s=S)
        info["predicted_rollout_state"] = rearrange(rollout_state, "(b s) ... -> b s ...", b=B, s=S)
        return info

    def get_cost(self, info_dict: dict, action_candidates: torch.Tensor):
        """Compute the cost of action candidates given an info dict with goal and initial state."""

        assert "goal" in info_dict, "goal not in info_dict"

        device = next(self.parameters()).device
        for k in list(info_dict.keys()):
            if torch.is_tensor(info_dict[k]):
                info_dict[k] = info_dict[k].to(device)

        goal = {k: v[:, 0] for k, v in info_dict.items() if torch.is_tensor(v)}
        goal["pixels"] = goal["goal"]

        for k in info_dict:
            if k.startswith("goal_"):
                goal[k[len("goal_") :]] = goal.pop(k)

        goal.pop("action")
        goal = self.encode(goal)

        info_dict["goal_emb"] = goal["emb"]
        info_dict["goal_emb_raw"] = goal["emb_raw"]
        info_dict = self.rollout(info_dict, action_candidates)

        cost = self.criterion(info_dict)
        return cost

    def criterion(self, info_dict: dict):
        """Configurable planning cost in raw or normalized space."""

        pred_emb = self._select_embedding_space(
            emb_raw=info_dict["predicted_emb_raw"],
            emb_norm=info_dict["predicted_emb"],
            space=self._get_cost_space(),

        goal_emb = self._select_embedding_space(

            emb_norm=info_dict["goal_emb"],
            space=self._get_cost_space(),
        )
        goal_emb = self._broadcast_goal_to_prediction(goal_emb, pred_emb)
```

````
看了最新代码，第 4 点可以排除：**LeWM 的 `dynamic_margin_head` 是会进 optimizer 的。**

依据是：

- 在 [train.py](/home/ag/projects/wm_exp/train.py:218) 创建的是 `JEPA(...)`
- 然后在 [train.py](/home/ag/projects/wm_exp/train.py:225)：

```python
world_model.dynamic_margin_head = nn.Linear(2 * embed_dim, 1)
```

- 这个发生在 [train.py](/home/ag/projects/wm_exp/train.py:240) 的 `spt.Module(model=world_model, ...)` 包装之前
- optimizer 配置是 [train.py](/home/ag/projects/wm_exp/train.py:231)：

```python
"modules": "model"
```

只要 `stable_pretraining` 的常规语义是优化 `self.model.parameters()`，这个 head 会作为 `JEPA` 的子模块被 PyTorch 注册进 `named_parameters()`。所以它不是 “没进 optimizer”。

更可能的问题不是第 4 点，而是 dynamic margin 在 LeWM 上退化了。尤其你现在的实现是：

```python
score = torch.sigmoid(raw_score)
score = score / score.detach().mean().clamp_min(1e-6)
margin = base_margin * score
```

如果 head 输出逐渐变成常数，那么 forward 里：

```text
score / mean(score) = 1
margin = base_margin
std = 0
```

这正好对应你看到的现象。

一个值得注意的细节：因为 denominator 是 `detach()` 的，**常数 bias 方向在 forward 上没有效果，但 backward 里仍可能被推着走**。如果 LeWM 的 hinge 梯度主要是 “所有 transition 都想增大 margin”，head 很可能主要更新 bias，raw score 变成更大的常数，sigmoid 逐渐饱和，最后 `margin_std` 还是 0，区分性梯度也更弱。

所以结论是：LeWM dynamic head 大概率不是没训练，而是训练成了常数解。下一步我建议直接 log：

```text
temporal_dist_p10/p50/p90
dynamic_raw_score_mean/std
dynamic_margin_head.weight.norm
dynamic_margin_head.bias
```

如果 `raw_score_std -> 0` 但 `bias` 在动，就能确认是 bias-only collapse。
````

### Assistant - 2026-04-27 04:27:30 EDT

````
那基本可以确定：**LeWM 的 dynamic margin head 已经退化成常数函数了**，不是 logging 问题，也不是 optimizer 没挂上。

现在链条是：

```text
dynamic_raw_score_std -> 0
temporal_margin_std -> 0
temporal_dist_p10/p50/p90 -> 常数/几乎不变
```

这说明两件事：

1. `dynamic_margin_head(z_t, a_t)` 没学到 transition-specific 差异。
2. LeWM 自己的相邻 latent distance 分布也被训练成了几乎固定尺度。

这和 SWM 不一样，因为 SWM 是 normalized sphere + uniformity/temporal_mask，局部距离仍有可变结构；LeWM 的 SIGReg + MSE pred 更容易把局部 transition norm 规整到一个固定壳层或固定步长。

我会优先怀疑这个机制：

```text
LeWM pred_loss:
  pred_emb ≈ emb_{t+1}

SIGReg:
  约束全局 embedding 分布

hinge_dynamic:
  只看到 ||z_{t+1} - z_t||
```

如果训练后所有 `||z_{t+1} - z_t||` 都差不多，那么 dynamic margin 没有东西可学。它最优就是输出一个常数 margin。

更关键的是：当前 dynamic head 输入是：

```python
margin_input = torch.cat([z_t.detach(), act_emb.detach()], dim=-1)
```

但监督信号本质来自：

```python
dist = ||z_{t+1} - z_t||
```

如果 LeWM 的 `dist` 和 `(z_t, a_t)` 相关性很弱，或者 `dist` 已经被整体几何压成常数，那 raw score 变常数是合理结果。

所以我现在不会继续把 LeWM + hinge_dynamic 当主线调参。它在 LeWM 上学不到动态性，说明这个 objective 对 LeWM 的 latent geometry 没提供额外信息。

更值得试的替代方案有两个：

**方案 A：去掉 dynamic，保留弱固定 hinge**

LeWM 上如果只是想加一点连续性，固定 loose margin 反而更诚实：

```text
weight=0.001
margin 用 temporal_dist_p50 或 p75 附近
```

不要期待 dynamic margin。

**方案 B：把 dynamic margin 改成 dist prediction auxiliary**

不要让 margin head 只通过 hinge 间接学习，而是显式预测真实 transition distance：

```text
d_t = ||sg(z_{t+1}) - sg(z_t)||

d_hat_t = softplus(head(z_t, a_t))

loss_dist = Huber(d_hat_t, d_t.detach())
margin_t = clamp(alpha * d_hat_t, min, max)
```

这样至少 head 有明确监督，不会那么容易变常数。然后 hinge 用这个 predicted margin：

```text
hinge = max(0, dist - margin_t)^2
```

但注意 `dist` target 要 detach，否则模型可能通过改变 embedding 尺度配合 head 作弊。

**方案 C：用 action magnitude / action embedding norm 做先验**

如果动作大小本身能解释突变，可以先不学复杂 head：

```text
margin_t = base + beta * ||a_t||
```

这比当前 learned head 更可解释，也能快速验证 “action-conditioned margin 是否有意义”。

我对当前现象的结论是：**不是 temporal_mask 混用的问题，而是 LeWM 的局部 transition distance 已经没有可学习的动态方差；dynamic hinge 在这个 geometry 上自然坍成 fixed hinge。**
````

````
| Reacher | 58.4 | 57.2 | -1.2 |
| Avg | 76.25 | 70.55 | **-5.70** |

所以即使 SWM 上 `hinge_dynamic` 没完全坍缩成 fixed margin，eval 也没有变好。最大问题是 TwoRoom 被严重打坏，Cube/PushT/Reacher 大体是小幅负向或接近持平。

LeWM 这边，表里只有 TwoRoom dynamic：

```text
tworoom_lewm: 93
tworoom_lewm_temporal_hinge_w001_m05: 100
tworoom_lewm_temporal_hinge_w001_dynamic_default: 100
```

但你前面说 LeWM dynamic 的 margin/std/raw_score 都坍成常数，所以这个结果更像是：**dynamic_default 退化成 fixed m≈0.5，然后复现了 fixed m05 在 TwoRoom 上的收益**。这不是 dynamic 学到了什么，而是 TwoRoom 刚好喜欢 weak loose continuity prior。

我对现象的解释是：

1. **TwoRoom 对 continuity prior 很敏感**
   LeWM fixed `w=0.001,m=0.5` 和 dynamic-default 都能到 100，但 SWM dynamic 却从 91 掉到 71.2。说明 SWM 当前 geometry 里 dynamic hinge 改坏了局部结构，可能影响规划距离或房间/门口附近的判别。

2. **Cube 基本不敏感**
   LeWM/SWM 的 fixed/dynamic 都在 70-74 附近波动。Cube 不应该作为筛选动态 hinge 的主指标，它区分度太弱。

3. **PushT 明确反对强 continuity**
   LeWM fixed hinge 直接崩到 6-20；SWM dynamic 虽然没有崩，但也没收益。PushT 的 contact/manipulation transition 不适合 “相邻 latent 必须近” 这个强先验。

4. **Reacher 小幅负向**
   SWM fixed m1 能到 60，比 58.4 略高，但 dynamic 是 57.2。说明如果有收益，也不是来自当前 dynamic 参数化。

**下一步建议**

不要继续 broad sweep `hinge_dynamic` 的 weight/base_margin/min/max。收益信号不够，而且 TwoRoom 的大负向说明风险很高。

我建议改成三步：

**1. 先保留主 baseline**

主线仍然用：

```text
SWM + BN + uniformity w=0.2 t=2 + temporal_masked_2 dim64
```

这是目前四任务平均最稳的 family。先做多 seed 确认，因为 2026-04-15 和 2026-04-25 的 PushT/Reacher 差异不小。

**2. 放弃当前 hinge_dynamic，改测 “诊断型” auxiliary**

不要让 margin head 通过 hinge 间接学，改成显式预测 transition distance：

```text
target_d = stopgrad(dist(z_t, z_{t+1}))
pred_d = softplus(head(z_t, a_t))
loss = Huber(pred_d, target_d)
```

先只作为 auxiliary log/aux loss，小权重，不拿它直接 clamp latent。目标是回答：`(z_t,a_t)` 到底能不能预测 transition distance。  
如果这个都学不好，dynamic margin 就没有理论基础。

**3. 如果还想做 continuity，用 gated 而不是 margin**

比 dynamic margin 更合理的是：

```text
gate_t * max(0, dist - margin)^2
```

其中 `gate_t` 学 “这一步是否应该连续”，而不是学 margin 大小。PushT 这种任务里，关键不是 margin 多大，而是有些 transition 根本不该被 continuity 约束。

优先实验矩阵我会排成：

```text
A. SWM baseline 多 seed
B. SWM + dist prediction auxiliary, no hinge
C. SWM + gated fixed hinge, weak weight
D. LeWM fixed loose hinge 只在 TwoRoom/Cube 做确认，不扩成主线
```

当前数据下，我会把 `hinge_dynamic` 标记为：**LeWM 上退化成 fixed hinge；SWM 上不退化但 eval 负向，尤其 TwoRoom 大幅负收益。暂停作为主方向。**
````

````
我检索了一圈后，结论是：**当前 hinge_dynamic 这条形式和业界效果好的路线不太一致**。相关工作里有效的东西，通常不是“给相邻状态一个可学习 margin 然后拉近”，而是下面几类：**latent dynamics prediction、bisimulation/behavioral distance、temporal distance/reachability、action/controllability-aware representation**。

结合你的实验，我建议下一步不要继续调 `hinge_dynamic`，而是换目标。

**最相关的几条线**

1. **DeepMDP / latent dynamics model**

DeepMDP 的核心是学 encoder 后，再学：

```text
p(z_{t+1} | z_t, a_t)
r_t = R(z_t, a_t)
```

它不是约束 `z_t` 和 `z_{t+1}` 一定近，而是要求 latent dynamics 可预测、reward/task 信息可保留。论文强调通过 next latent distribution prediction 和 reward prediction 学有用抽象表示，并和 bisimulation 有理论联系。见 ICML 2019 DeepMDP: [DeepMDP](https://icml.cc/virtual/2019/oral/5163)。

这对你很关键：**动态性不应该通过 “距离上界” 学，而应该通过 “action-conditioned transition 可预测性” 学。**

2. **DBC / Bisimulation metric**

DBC 直接把 latent 距离对齐到 behavioral similarity：

```text
d(s_i, s_j) ≈ reward difference + transition distribution difference
```

也就是说，两个状态近不近，不由时间相邻决定，而由“它们在控制上是否等价”决定。DBC 在视觉控制里专门强调不用 reconstruction，学习 task-relevant representation，并在带 distractor 的 MuJoCo/CARLA 任务上有效。见 ICLR 2021 oral: [Learning Invariant Representations for RL without Reconstruction](https://iclr.cc/virtual/2021/oral/3480)。

这比 hinge 更适合解释你的现象：PushT/Reacher 里相邻状态不一定应该近，**控制等价性才重要**。

3. **MICo / sampling-based state similarity**

MICo 是 Google/DeepMind 方向的 behavioral distance 简化版，目标也是用 MDP state similarity shaping representation，并报告 Atari 上有强结果。见 NeurIPS 2021: [MICo](https://research.google/pubs/mico-improved-representations-via-sampling-based-state-similarity-for-markov-decision-processes/)。

它的启发是：如果要做 pairwise distance loss，别用 `t,t+1` 的物理邻接，而要用 **behavioral distance target**。

4. **TD-MPC / TD-MPC2**

TD-MPC2 是近年很强的 decoder-free latent world model 路线。它在 latent space 里做 model predictive control，不解码像素，强调 task-oriented latent dynamics。TD-MPC2 在 104 个 continuous-control tasks 上单套超参表现强。见官网: [TD-MPC2](https://www.tdmpc2.com/)。

这和你的 SWM/LeWM 很相关：你们也是 decoder-free planning latent。TD-MPC2 的经验说明，强路线是：

```text
latent dynamics + reward/value prediction + planning objective
```

而不是单纯 latent continuity。

5. **Temporal Distance-aware Representations**

TLDR/TempDATA 这类工作显式学 temporal distance/reachability，用“几步能到”作为 representation signal，而不是把相邻状态都拉近。TLDR 在机器人 locomotion 的 unsupervised goal-conditioned RL 上报告明显优于 prior methods。见 CoRL 2024/PMLR: [TLDR](https://proceedings.mlr.press/v270/bae25a.html)。TempDATA 也用 temporal-distance representation 做 offline model-based RL transition augmentation: [TempDATA](https://proceedings.mlr.press/v267/lee25p.html)。

这给你的启发是：如果你关心“近/远/突变/连续”，更自然的是学：

```text
D(z_i, z_j) ≈ temporal steps / reachability
```

而不是：

```text
dist(z_t, z_{t+1}) <= margin_t
```

**对当前结果的判断**

你的 `hinge_dynamic` 失败不是偶然。它的 inductive bias 是：

```text
相邻 transition 应该有一个可学习距离上限
```

但业界有效路线更像：

```text
状态距离应该反映 dynamics / reward / value / reachability / controllability
```

这两者不一样。特别是 PushT、Reacher、contact dynamics 里，相邻状态的 embedding 不一定该近；有些关键接触、小幅动作、门口切换、目标接近阶段，latent 需要放大差异。

**我建议下一步优先做 3 个实验**

1. **Dist prediction auxiliary，不再反向约束 embedding**

先验证 `(z_t, a_t)` 是否能预测真实 transition distance：

```text
target = stopgrad(dist(z_t, z_{t+1}))
pred = softplus(head(z_t, a_t))
loss = Huber(pred, target)
```

只作为 auxiliary 和诊断，不拿 `pred` 去 clamp embedding。  
如果这个任务都学不好，dynamic margin 没必要继续。

2. **Inverse dynamics auxiliary**

加一个轻量 head：

```text
a_hat = g(z_t, z_{t+1})
loss_inv = MSE(a_hat, a_t)
```

这个更贴近 controllability/action-aware representation。尤其 PushT/Reacher，应该比 continuity hinge 更合理。

3. **Temporal distance / reachability head**

采样同一 trajectory 内 `(z_i, z_j)`，预测 gap bin：

```text
gap = clamp(|i-j|, max_k)
pred_gap = h(z_i, z_j)
loss = CE(pred_gap, gap_bin)
```

或者用 contrastive ranking：

```text
same trajectory closer-in-time pair should score closer than farther pair
```

这能保留“时间结构”，但不会强迫每个 `t,t+1` 都贴得很近。

我的排序是：

```text
先做 inverse dynamics auxiliary
再做 dist prediction diagnostic
最后做 temporal distance head
```

原因是 inverse dynamics 最便宜、最贴近 action-conditioned control；dist prediction 用来判断 dynamic margin 的前提是否成立；temporal distance 更像一个新 representation branch，实验成本稍高。

当前最不建议继续的是：

```text
hinge_dynamic weight/base_margin/min/max sweep
```

已有结果说明它在 SWM 上整体负向，在 LeWM 上退化成 fixed hinge。这个方向的失败信号已经足够强。
````

```
import torch
from torch import nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
from einops import rearrange


def modulate(x, shift, scale):
    """AdaLN-zero modulation"""
    return x * (1 + scale) + shift


class SIGReg(torch.nn.Module):
    """Sketch Isotropic Gaussian Regularizer (single-GPU!)"""

    def __init__(self, knots=17, num_proj=1024):
        super().__init__()
        self.num_proj = num_proj
        t = torch.linspace(0, 3, knots, dtype=torch.float32)
        dt = 3 / (knots - 1)
        weights = torch.full((knots,), 2 * dt, dtype=torch.float32)
        weights[[0, -1]] = dt
        window = torch.exp(-t.square() / 2.0)
        self.register_buffer("t", t)
        self.register_buffer("phi", window)
        self.register_buffer("weights", weights * window)

    def forward(self, proj):
        """
        proj: (T, B, D)
        """
        # sample random projections
        A = torch.randn(proj.size(-1), self.num_proj, device=proj.device)
        A = A.div_(A.norm(p=2, dim=0))
        # compute the epps-pulley statistic
        x_t = (proj @ A).unsqueeze(-1) * self.t
        err = (x_t.cos().mean(-3) - self.phi).square() + x_t.sin().mean(-3).square()
        statistic = (err @ self.weights) * proj.size(-2)
        return statistic.mean()  # average over projections and time


class FeedForward(nn.Module):
    """FeedForward network used in Transformers"""

    def __init__(self, dim, hidden_dim, dropout=0.0):
        super().__init__()
        self.net = nn.Sequential(
            nn.LayerNorm(dim),
            nn.Linear(dim, hidden_dim),
            nn.GELU(),
            nn.Dropout(dropout),
            nn.Linear(hidden_dim, dim),
            nn.Dropout(dropout),
        )

    def forward(self, x):
        return self.net(x)


class Attention(nn.Module):
    """Scaled dot-product attention with causal masking"""

    def __init__(self, dim, heads=8, dim_head=64, dropout=0.0):
        super().__init__()
        inner_dim = dim_head * heads
        project_out = not (heads == 1 and dim_head == dim)
        self.heads = heads
        self.scale = dim_head**-0.5
        self.dropout = dropout
        self.norm = nn.LayerNorm(dim)
        self.attend = nn.Softmax(dim=-1)
        self.to_qkv = nn.Linear(dim, inner_dim * 3, bias=False)
        self.to_out = (
            nn.Sequential(nn.Linear(inner_dim, dim), nn.Dropout(dropout))
            if project_out
            else nn.Identity()
        )

    def forward(self, x, causal=True):
        """
        x : (B, T, D)
        """
        x = self.norm(x)
        drop = self.dropout if self.training else 0.0
        qkv = self.to_qkv(x).chunk(3, dim=-1)  # q, k, v: (B, heads, T, dim_head)
        q, k, v = (rearrange(t, "b t (h d) -> b h t d", h=self.heads) for t in qkv)
        out = F.scaled_dot_product_attention(q, k, v, dropout_p=drop, is_causal=causal)
        out = rearrange(out, "b h t d -> b t (h d)")
        return self.to_out(out)


class ConditionalBlock(nn.Module):
    """Transformer block with AdaLN-zero conditioning"""

    def __init__(self, dim, heads, dim_head, mlp_dim, dropout=0.0):
        super().__init__()

        self.attn = Attention(dim, heads=heads, dim_head=dim_head, dropout=dropout)
        self.mlp = FeedForward(dim, mlp_dim, dropout=dropout)
        self.norm1 = nn.LayerNorm(dim, elementwise_affine=False, eps=1e-6)
        self.norm2 = nn.LayerNorm(dim, elementwise_affine=False, eps=1e-6)
        self.adaLN_modulation = nn.Sequential(
            nn.SiLU(), nn.Linear(dim, 6 * dim, bias=True)
        )

        nn.init.constant_(self.adaLN_modulation[-1].weight, 0)
        nn.init.constant_(self.adaLN_modulation[-1].bias, 0)

    def forward(self, x, c):
        shift_msa, scale_msa, gate_msa, shift_mlp, scale_mlp, gate_mlp = (
            self.adaLN_modulation(c).chunk(6, dim=-1)
        )
        x = x + gate_msa * self.attn(modulate(self.norm1(x), shift_msa, scale_msa))
        x = x + gate_mlp * self.mlp(modulate(self.norm2(x), shift_mlp, scale_mlp))
        return x


class Block(nn.Module):
    """Standard Transformer block"""

    def __init__(self, dim, heads, dim_head, mlp_dim, dropout=0.0):
        super().__init__()

        self.attn = Attention(dim, heads=heads, dim_head=dim_head, dropout=dropout)
        self.mlp = FeedForward(dim, mlp_dim, dropout=dropout)
        self.norm1 = nn.LayerNorm(dim, elementwise_affine=False, eps=1e-6)
        self.norm2 = nn.LayerNorm(dim, elementwise_affine=False, eps=1e-6)

    def forward(self, x):
        x = x + self.attn(self.norm1(x))
        x = x + self.mlp(self.norm2(x))
        return x


class Transformer(nn.Module):
    """Standard Transformer with support for AdaLN-zero blocks"""

    def __init__(
        self,
        input_dim,
        hidden_dim,
        output_dim,
        depth,
        heads,
        dim_head,
        mlp_dim,
        dropout=0.0,
        block_class=Block,
    ):
        super().__init__()
        self.norm = nn.LayerNorm(hidden_dim)
        self.layers = nn.ModuleList([])

        self.input_proj = (
            nn.Linear(input_dim, hidden_dim)
            if input_dim != hidden_dim
            else nn.Identity()
        )

        self.cond_proj = (
            nn.Linear(input_dim, hidden_dim)
            if input_dim != hidden_dim
            else nn.Identity()
        )

        self.output_proj = (
            nn.Linear(hidden_dim, output_dim)
            if hidden_dim != output_dim
            else nn.Identity()
        )

        for _ in range(depth):
            self.layers.append(
                block_class(hidden_dim, heads, dim_head, mlp_dim, dropout)
            )

    def forward(self, x, c=None):

        if hasattr(self, "input_proj"):
            x = self.input_proj(x)
```

```
class Embedder(nn.Module):
    def __init__(
        self,
        input_dim=10,
        smoothed_dim=10,
        emb_dim=10,
        mlp_scale=4,
    ):
        super().__init__()
        self.patch_embed = nn.Conv1d(input_dim, smoothed_dim, kernel_size=1, stride=1)
        self.embed = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Linear(smoothed_dim, mlp_scale * emb_dim),
            nn.SiLU(),
            nn.Linear(mlp_scale * emb_dim, emb_dim),
        )

    def forward(self, x):
        """
        x: (B, T, D)
        """
        x = x.float()
        x = x.permute(0, 2, 1)
        x = self.patch_embed(x)
        x = x.permute(0, 2, 1)
        x = self.embed(x)
        return x


class MLP(nn.Module):
    """Simple MLP with optional normalization and activation"""

    def __init__(
        self,
        input_dim,
        hidden_dim,
        output_dim=None,
        norm_fn=nn.LayerNorm,
        act_fn=nn.GELU,
    ):
        super().__init__()
        norm_fn = norm_fn(hidden_dim) if norm_fn is not None else nn.Identity()
        self.net = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Linear(input_dim, hidden_dim),
            norm_fn,
            act_fn(),
            nn.Linear(hidden_dim, output_dim or input_dim),
        )

    def forward(self, x):
        """
        x: (B*T, D)
        """
        return self.net(x)


def cosine_pred_loss(pred: torch.Tensor, target: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
    """Prediction loss on the sphere: mean cosine distance.

    pred, target: (B, T, D) — unit vectors on S^{d-1}.
    Returns scalar in [0, 2]; 0 means perfect alignment.
    """
    return (1.0 - (pred * target).sum(dim=-1)).mean()


def temporal_hinge_loss(
    z_t: torch.Tensor,
    z_tp1: torch.Tensor,
    *,
    margin: float,
    metric: str,
    squared: bool = True,
) -> torch.Tensor:
    """Upper hinge loss on consecutive latent pairs.

    z_t, z_tp1: (..., D) consecutive latent embeddings with matching shapes.
    metric:
      - "l2": Euclidean distance in R^d
      - "cosine": cosine distance 1 - cos(z_t, z_tp1), suitable for unit vectors

    This encourages consecutive latents to stay close, but stops rewarding
    them once their distance is already below the margin:
      max(0, distance(z_t, z_{t+1}) - margin)
    """
    if z_t.shape != z_tp1.shape:
        raise ValueError(
            f"z_t and z_tp1 must have the same shape, got {z_t.shape} vs {z_tp1.shape}"
        )
    if margin < 0:
        raise ValueError(f"margin must be >= 0, got {margin}")

    if metric == "l2":
        dist = torch.linalg.vector_norm(z_tp1 - z_t, dim=-1)
    elif metric == "cosine":
        z_t = F.normalize(z_t, dim=-1, eps=1e-8)
        z_tp1 = F.normalize(z_tp1, dim=-1, eps=1e-8)
        dist = 1.0 - (z_t * z_tp1).sum(dim=-1)
    else:
        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported temporal hinge metric: {metric}")

    loss = torch.clamp_min(dist - margin, 0.0)
    if squared:
        loss = loss.square()
    return loss.mean()


def temporal_straightness(z: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
    """Mean cosine between consecutive latent displacement vectors.

    z: (B, T, D) encoder outputs on real trajectories.
    Returns a scalar; larger values mean temporally straighter trajectories.
    """
    if z.ndim != 3:
        raise ValueError(f"z must have shape (B, T, D), got {z.shape}")
    if z.size(1) < 3:
        return z.new_tensor(0.0)

    v = z[:, 1:] - z[:, :-1]  # (B, T-1, D)
    v1 = v[:, :-1]  # (B, T-2, D)
    v2 = v[:, 1:]  # (B, T-2, D)

    denom = v1.norm(dim=-1) * v2.norm(dim=-1) + 1e-8
    cos = (v1 * v2).sum(dim=-1) / denom
    return cos.mean()


def _pairwise_offdiag(x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
    """Return all off-diagonal pairwise entries for a flattened batch."""
    n = x.size(0)
    mask = ~torch.eye(n, dtype=torch.bool, device=x.device)
    return x[mask]


def spread_loss(emb: torch.Tensor, margin: float) -> torch.Tensor:
    """Anti-collapse loss: mean squared hinge on pairwise cosine similarity.

    emb: (B, T, D) — unit vectors on S^{d-1}.
    Computes mean_{i != j} max(0, <mu_i, mu_j> - m)^2.
    Minimising this only penalises pairs whose cosine similarity exceeds the
    configured margin.
    """
    z = emb.reshape(-1, emb.size(-1))  # (B*T, D)
    sim = z @ z.T  # (B*T, B*T)
    return torch.clamp_min(_pairwise_offdiag(sim) - margin, 0.0).square().mean()


def _uniformity_pair_mask(
    batch_size: int,
    seq_len: int,
    *,
    mode: str,
    temporal_exclusion: int,
    device,
) -> torch.Tensor:
    """Return the pair mask used by uniformity regularization."""
    if temporal_exclusion < 0:
        raise ValueError("uniformity temporal_exclusion must be >= 0")

    n = batch_size * seq_len
    upper = torch.triu(torch.ones(n, n, dtype=torch.bool, device=device), diagonal=1)
    if mode == "all_pairs":
        return upper

    batch_ids = torch.arange(batch_size, device=device).repeat_interleave(seq_len)
    if mode == "cross_window":
        return upper & (batch_ids[:, None] != batch_ids[None, :])

    if mode == "temporal_masked":
        time_ids = torch.arange(seq_len, device=device).repeat(batch_size)
        near_temporal = (batch_ids[:, None] == batch_ids[None, :]) & (
            (time_ids[:, None] - time_ids[None, :]).abs() <= temporal_exclusion
        )
        return upper & ~near_temporal

    raise ValueError(f"Unsupported uniformity.mode: {mode}")


def uniformity_loss(
    emb: torch.Tensor,
    t: float,
    *,
    mode: str = "all_pairs",
    temporal_exclusion: int = 0,
) -> torch.Tensor:
    """Wang & Isola uniformity loss on unit-normalized embeddings.

    emb: (B, T, D) — unit vectors on S^{d-1}.
    Computes log mean exp(-t * ||mu_i - mu_j||^2) over a configurable subset
    of flattened batch-time pairs.
    """
    z = emb.reshape(-1, emb.size(-1))  # (B*T, D)
    if z.size(0) < 2:
        return z.new_tensor(0.0)

    sq_dists = torch.cdist(z, z, p=2).square()
    mask = _uniformity_pair_mask(
        emb.size(0),
        emb.size(1),
        mode=mode.lower(),
        temporal_exclusion=temporal_exclusion,
        device=z.device,
    )
    sq_dists = sq_dists[mask]
    if sq_dists.numel() == 0:
        return z.new_tensor(0.0)
    return torch.exp(-t * sq_dists).mean().log()


def infonce_loss(
    pred: torch.Tensor, target: torch.Tensor, temperature: float
) -> torch.Tensor:
    """Symmetric batch-wise InfoNCE for next-step prediction.

    pred: (B, T, D) predicted next-step embeddings
    target: (B, T, D) ground-truth next-step embeddings

    For each time step t and batch item b, this uses the aligned pair
    (pred[b, t], target[b, t]) as the positive. The denominator contains:
    - all cross-view samples at the same time step, and
    - all same-view samples except self.

    The loss is symmetric:
      InfoNCE(pred, target) + InfoNCE(target, pred)
    averaged over batch and time.
    """
    if pred.shape != target.shape:
        raise ValueError(
            f"pred and target must have the same shape, got {pred.shape} vs {target.shape}"
        )
    if temperature <= 0:
        raise ValueError(f"temperature must be > 0, got {temperature}")

    batch_size = pred.size(0)
    if batch_size < 2:
        return pred.new_tensor(0.0)

    q = F.normalize(pred, dim=-1, eps=1e-8)
    k = F.normalize(target, dim=-1, eps=1e-8)
```

```
import os
from functools import partial
from pathlib import Path

import hydra
import lightning as pl
import stable_pretraining as spt
import stable_worldmodel as swm
import torch
from lightning.pytorch.loggers import WandbLogger
from torch import nn

try:
    from swanlab.integration.pytorch_lightning import SwanLabLogger
except ImportError:
    SwanLabLogger = None
from omegaconf import OmegaConf, open_dict

from jepa import JEPA
from module import (
    ARPredictor,
    Embedder,
    MLP,
    SIGReg,
    temporal_hinge_loss,
    temporal_straightness,
)
from utils import get_column_normalizer, get_img_preprocessor, ModelObjectCallBack


def compute_temporal_hinge(output, *, model, cfg):
    """Upper hinge loss on consecutive latent pairs (LeWM variant).

    Mirrors the SWM compute_temporal_hinge exactly, except distance is
    computed with L2 (Euclidean) instead of cosine because LeWM does not
    L2-normalise its embeddings.
    """
    emb = output["emb"]
    if emb.size(1) <= 1:
        output["temporal_hinge_active_ratio"] = emb.new_tensor(0.0)
        return emb.new_tensor(0.0)

    hinge_cfg = cfg.loss.temporal_hinge
    dynamic_cfg = hinge_cfg.get("dynamic", {})
    z_t = emb[:, :-1]
    z_tp1 = emb[:, 1:]

    if not dynamic_cfg.get("enabled", False):
        dist = torch.linalg.vector_norm(z_tp1 - z_t, dim=-1)
        margin = hinge_cfg.margin
        hinge = torch.clamp_min(dist - margin, 0.0)
        output["temporal_hinge_active_ratio"] = (hinge > 0).float().mean()
        if hinge_cfg.squared:
            hinge = hinge.square()
        return hinge.mean()

    if not hasattr(model, "dynamic_margin_head"):
        raise AttributeError(
            "loss.temporal_hinge.dynamic.enabled=True requires "
            "model.dynamic_margin_head to be initialized"
        )

    act_emb = output["act_emb"][:, :-1]
    margin_input = torch.cat([z_t.detach(), act_emb.detach()], dim=-1)
    raw_score = model.dynamic_margin_head(margin_input).squeeze(-1)
    score = torch.sigmoid(raw_score)
    score = score / score.detach().mean().clamp_min(1e-6)

    margin = dynamic_cfg.get("base_margin", hinge_cfg.margin) * score
    margin = margin.clamp(
        min=dynamic_cfg.get("min_margin", 0.05),
        max=dynamic_cfg.get("max_margin", 1.0),
    )

    dist = torch.linalg.vector_norm(z_tp1 - z_t, dim=-1)
    hinge = torch.clamp_min(dist - margin, 0.0)
    output["temporal_hinge_active_ratio"] = (hinge > 0).float().mean()
    output["temporal_margin_mean"] = margin.mean()
    output["temporal_margin_std"] = margin.std(unbiased=False)
    margin_flat = margin.detach().float().flatten()
    output["temporal_margin_p10"] = torch.quantile(margin_flat, 0.10)
    output["temporal_margin_p50"] = torch.quantile(margin_flat, 0.50)
    output["temporal_margin_p90"] = torch.quantile(margin_flat, 0.90)

    if hinge_cfg.squared:
        hinge = hinge.square()
    return hinge.mean()


def lejepa_forward(self, batch, stage, cfg):
    """encode observations, predict next states, compute losses."""

    ctx_len = cfg.wm.history_size
    n_preds = cfg.wm.num_preds
    sigreg_lambd = cfg.loss.sigreg.weight
    hinge_cfg = cfg.loss.temporal_hinge

    # Replace NaN values with 0 (occurs at sequence boundaries)
    batch["action"] = torch.nan_to_num(batch["action"], 0.0)

    output = self.model.encode(batch)

    emb = output["emb"]  # (B, T, D)
    act_emb = output["act_emb"]

    ctx_emb = emb[:, :ctx_len]
    ctx_act = act_emb[:, :ctx_len]

    tgt_emb = emb[:, n_preds:]  # label
    pred_emb = self.model.predict(ctx_emb, ctx_act)  # pred

    # LeWM loss
    output["pred_loss"] = (pred_emb - tgt_emb).pow(2).mean()
    output["sigreg_loss"] = self.sigreg(emb.transpose(0, 1))
    output["temporal_hinge_loss"] = compute_temporal_hinge(
        output, model=self.model, cfg=cfg
    )
    output["loss"] = (
        output["pred_loss"]
        + sigreg_lambd * output["sigreg_loss"]
        + hinge_cfg.weight * output["temporal_hinge_loss"]
    )
    output["temporal_straightness"] = temporal_straightness(emb)

    losses_dict = {f"{stage}/{k}": v.detach() for k, v in output.items() if "loss" in k}
    metrics_dict = {
        f"{stage}/{k}": v.detach()
        for k, v in output.items()
        if (
            torch.is_tensor(v)
            and (
                k == "temporal_straightness"
                or k == "temporal_hinge_active_ratio"
                or k.startswith("temporal_margin_")
            )
        )
    }
    self.log_dict({**losses_dict, **metrics_dict}, on_step=True, sync_dist=True)
    return output


@hydra.main(version_base=None, config_path="./config/train", config_name="lewm")
def run(cfg):
    #########################
    ##       dataset       ##
    #########################

    dataset = swm.data.HDF5Dataset(**cfg.data.dataset, transform=None)
    transforms = [
        get_img_preprocessor(source="pixels", target="pixels", img_size=cfg.img_size)
    ]

    with open_dict(cfg):
        for col in cfg.data.dataset.keys_to_load:
            if col.startswith("pixels"):
                continue

            normalizer = get_column_normalizer(dataset, col, col)
            transforms.append(normalizer)

            setattr(cfg.wm, f"{col}_dim", dataset.get_dim(col))

    transform = spt.data.transforms.Compose(*transforms)
    dataset.transform = transform

    rnd_gen = torch.Generator().manual_seed(cfg.seed)
    train_set, val_set = spt.data.random_split(
        dataset, lengths=[cfg.train_split, 1 - cfg.train_split], generator=rnd_gen
    )

    train = torch.utils.data.DataLoader(
        train_set, **cfg.loader, shuffle=True, drop_last=True, generator=rnd_gen
    )
    val = torch.utils.data.DataLoader(
        val_set, **cfg.loader, shuffle=False, drop_last=False
    )

    ##############################
    ##       model / optim      ##
    ##############################

    encoder = spt.backbone.utils.vit_hf(
        cfg.encoder_scale,
        patch_size=cfg.patch_size,
        image_size=cfg.img_size,
        pretrained=False,
        use_mask_token=False,
    )

    hidden_dim = encoder.config.hidden_size
    embed_dim = cfg.wm.get("embed_dim", hidden_dim)
    effective_act_dim = cfg.data.dataset.frameskip * cfg.wm.action_dim

    predictor = ARPredictor(
        num_frames=cfg.wm.history_size,
        input_dim=embed_dim,
        hidden_dim=hidden_dim,
        output_dim=hidden_dim,
        **cfg.predictor,
    )

    action_encoder = Embedder(input_dim=effective_act_dim, emb_dim=embed_dim)

    projector = MLP(
        input_dim=hidden_dim,
        output_dim=embed_dim,
        hidden_dim=2048,
        norm_fn=torch.nn.BatchNorm1d,
    )

    predictor_proj = MLP(
        input_dim=hidden_dim,
        output_dim=embed_dim,
        hidden_dim=2048,
        norm_fn=torch.nn.BatchNorm1d,
    )

    world_model = JEPA(
        encoder=encoder,
        predictor=predictor,
        action_encoder=action_encoder,
        projector=projector,
        pred_proj=predictor_proj,
    )
    if cfg.loss.temporal_hinge.get("dynamic", {}).get("enabled", False):
        world_model.dynamic_margin_head = nn.Linear(2 * embed_dim, 1)
        nn.init.zeros_(world_model.dynamic_margin_head.weight)
        nn.init.zeros_(world_model.dynamic_margin_head.bias)

    optimizers = {
        "model_opt": {
            "modules": "model",
            "optimizer": dict(cfg.optimizer),
            "scheduler": {"type": "LinearWarmupCosineAnnealingLR"},
            "interval": "epoch",
        },
    }

    data_module = spt.data.DataModule(train=train, val=val)
    world_model = spt.Module(
        model=world_model,
        sigreg=SIGReg(**cfg.loss.sigreg.kwargs),
        forward=partial(lejepa_forward, cfg=cfg),
        optim=optimizers,
    )

    ##########################
    ##       training       ##
    ##########################

    run_id = cfg.get("subdir") or ""
    run_dir = Path(swm.data.utils.get_cache_dir(), run_id)

    logger = None
    backend = cfg.get("logger_backend", "swanlab")
    if backend == "swanlab" and cfg.swanlab.enabled:
        if SwanLabLogger is None:
            raise ImportError("swanlab is not installed. Run: pip install swanlab")
        logger = SwanLabLogger(**cfg.swanlab.config)
        logger.log_hyperparams(OmegaConf.to_container(cfg))
    elif backend == "wandb" and cfg.wandb.enabled:
        logger = WandbLogger(**cfg.wandb.config)
        logger.log_hyperparams(OmegaConf.to_container(cfg))

    run_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    with open(run_dir / "config.yaml", "w") as f:
        OmegaConf.save(cfg, f)

    object_dump_callback = ModelObjectCallBack(
        dirpath=run_dir,
        filename=cfg.output_model_name,
        epoch_interval=1,
    )

    trainer = pl.Trainer(
        **cfg.trainer,
        callbacks=[object_dump_callback],
        num_sanity_val_steps=1,
        logger=logger,
        enable_checkpointing=True,
    )

    manager = spt.Manager(
        trainer=trainer,
        module=world_model,
        data=data_module,
        ckpt_path=run_dir / f"{cfg.output_model_name}_weights.ckpt",
    )

    manager()
    return


if __name__ == "__main__":
    run()
```

```
import os
from functools import partial
from pathlib import Path

import hydra
import lightning as pl
import stable_pretraining as spt
import stable_worldmodel as swm
import torch
import torch.nn.functional as F
from lightning.pytorch.loggers import WandbLogger

try:
    from swanlab.integration.pytorch_lightning import SwanLabLogger
except ImportError:
    SwanLabLogger = None
from omegaconf import OmegaConf, open_dict
from torch import nn

from jepa import SphericalJEPA
from module import (
    ARPredictor,
    Embedder,
    MLP,
    infonce_loss,
    spread_loss,
    temporal_straightness,
    uniformity_loss,
)
from utils import get_column_normalizer, get_img_preprocessor, ModelObjectCallBack


def resolve_norm_fn(norm_name: str):
    norm_name = norm_name.lower()
    if norm_name in {"none", "identity"}:
        return None
    if norm_name in {"ln", "layernorm"}:
        return nn.LayerNorm
    if norm_name in {"bn", "batchnorm", "batchnorm1d"}:
        return nn.BatchNorm1d
    raise ValueError(f"Unsupported projection_head.norm_fn: {norm_name}")


def build_projection_head(input_dim: int, output_dim: int, cfg) -> nn.Module:
    head_type = cfg.type.lower()
    norm_name = cfg.get("norm_fn", "none")

    # Backward-compatible aliases for older configs.
    if head_type == "bn":
        head_type = "linear"
        norm_name = "batchnorm1d"
    elif head_type == "ln":
        head_type = "linear"
        norm_name = "layernorm"

    norm_fn = resolve_norm_fn(norm_name)

    if head_type == "linear":
        layers = [nn.Linear(input_dim, output_dim)]
        if norm_fn is not None:
            layers.append(norm_fn(output_dim))
        return nn.Sequential(*layers) if len(layers) > 1 else layers[0]

    if head_type == "mlp":
        return MLP(
            input_dim=input_dim,
            hidden_dim=cfg.get("hidden_dim", 2048),
            output_dim=output_dim,
            norm_fn=norm_fn,
        )

    raise ValueError(f"Unsupported projection_head.type: {head_type}")


def get_loss_space_tensors(output, *, pred_raw, pred_norm, n_preds: int, space: str):
    pred_len = pred_raw.size(1)
    space = space.lower()
    if space == "raw":
        return pred_raw, output["emb_raw"][:, n_preds : n_preds + pred_len]
    if space in {"normalized", "sphere"}:
        return pred_norm, output["emb"][:, n_preds : n_preds + pred_len]
    raise ValueError(f"Unsupported loss space: {space}")


def get_regularizer_tensor(output, *, space: str):
    space = space.lower()
    if space == "raw":
        return output["emb_raw"]
    if space in {"normalized", "sphere"}:
        return output["emb"]
    raise ValueError(f"Unsupported loss.regularizer.space: {space}")


def get_context_tensor(output, *, space: str):
    space = space.lower()
    if space == "raw":
        return output["emb_raw"]
    if space in {"normalized", "sphere"}:
        return output["emb"]
    raise ValueError(f"Unsupported loss.pred.context_space: {space}")


def get_embedding_tensor(output, *, space: str):
    space = space.lower()
    if space == "raw":
        return output["emb_raw"]
    if space in {"normalized", "sphere"}:
        return output["emb"]
    raise ValueError(f"Unsupported embedding space: {space}")


def compute_embedding_loss(
    pred: torch.Tensor,
    target: torch.Tensor,
    *,
    loss_type: str,
) -> torch.Tensor:
    loss_type = loss_type.lower()
    if loss_type == "cosine":
        return (1.0 - F.cosine_similarity(pred, target, dim=-1)).mean()
    if loss_type == "mse":
        return F.mse_loss(pred, target)
    raise ValueError(f"Unsupported prediction loss type: {loss_type}")


def compute_pred_loss(pred: torch.Tensor, target: torch.Tensor, cfg) -> torch.Tensor:
    pred_type = cfg.loss.pred.get("type", "cosine")
    return compute_embedding_loss(pred, target, loss_type=pred_type)


def compute_multistep_rollout_loss(output, *, model, cfg):
    rollout_cfg = cfg.loss.get("rollout", {})
    rollout_steps = int(rollout_cfg.get("steps", 1))
    if rollout_cfg.get("weight", 0.0) <= 0.0 or rollout_steps <= 1:
        return None

    ctx_len = cfg.wm.history_size
    available_future = output["emb"].size(1) - ctx_len
    rollout_steps = min(rollout_steps, available_future)
    if rollout_steps <= 1:
        return None

    context_space = rollout_cfg.get("context_space", cfg.loss.pred.get("context_space", cfg.loss.pred.get("space", "normalized")))
    target_space = rollout_cfg.get("space", cfg.loss.pred.get("space", "normalized"))
    loss_type = rollout_cfg.get("type", cfg.loss.pred.get("type", "cosine"))

    full_act_emb = output["act_emb"][:, : ctx_len + rollout_steps]
    rollout_raw = output["emb_raw"][:, :ctx_len].clone()
    rollout_norm = output["emb"][:, :ctx_len].clone()
    target = get_embedding_tensor(output, space=target_space)[:, ctx_len : ctx_len + rollout_steps]

    pred_steps = []
    for step in range(rollout_steps):
        action_end = ctx_len + step
        window = min(ctx_len, action_end)
        rollout_ctx = get_embedding_tensor(
            {"emb_raw": rollout_raw, "emb": rollout_norm},
            space=context_space,
        )[:, -window:]
        act_trunc = full_act_emb[:, action_end - window : action_end]

        pred_raw = model.predict_raw(rollout_ctx, act_trunc)[:, -1:]
        pred_norm = model.normalize_embeddings(pred_raw)
        pred_steps.append(
            get_embedding_tensor({"emb_raw": pred_raw, "emb": pred_norm}, space=target_space)
        )

        rollout_raw = torch.cat([rollout_raw, pred_raw], dim=1)
        rollout_norm = torch.cat([rollout_norm, pred_norm], dim=1)

    if len(pred_steps) <= 1:
        return None

    # Keep the first rollout step only as autoregressive context so this
    # auxiliary term supervises strictly beyond the one-step pred_loss.
    pred = torch.cat(pred_steps[1:], dim=1)
    target = target[:, 1:]
    return compute_embedding_loss(pred, target, loss_type=loss_type)


def compute_temporal_hinge(output, *, model, cfg):
    emb = output["emb"]
    if emb.size(1) <= 1:
        output["temporal_hinge_active_ratio"] = emb.new_tensor(0.0)
        return emb.new_tensor(0.0)

    hinge_cfg = cfg.loss.temporal_hinge
    dynamic_cfg = hinge_cfg.get("dynamic", {})
    z_t = emb[:, :-1]
    z_tp1 = emb[:, 1:]

    if not dynamic_cfg.get("enabled", False):
        dist = 1.0 - (z_t * z_tp1).sum(dim=-1)
        margin = hinge_cfg.margin
        hinge = torch.clamp_min(dist - margin, 0.0)
        output["temporal_hinge_active_ratio"] = (hinge > 0).float().mean()
        if hinge_cfg.squared:
            hinge = hinge.square()
        return hinge.mean()

    if not hasattr(model, "dynamic_margin_head"):
        raise AttributeError(
            "loss.temporal_hinge.dynamic.enabled=True requires "
            "model.dynamic_margin_head to be initialized"
        )

    act_emb = output["act_emb"][:, :-1]
    margin_input = torch.cat([z_t.detach(), act_emb.detach()], dim=-1)
    raw_score = model.dynamic_margin_head(margin_input).squeeze(-1)
    score = torch.sigmoid(raw_score)
    score = score / score.detach().mean().clamp_min(1e-6)

    margin = dynamic_cfg.get("base_margin", hinge_cfg.margin) * score
    margin = margin.clamp(
        min=dynamic_cfg.get("min_margin", 0.05),
        max=dynamic_cfg.get("max_margin", 1.0),
    )

    dist = 1.0 - (z_t * z_tp1).sum(dim=-1)
    hinge = torch.clamp_min(dist - margin, 0.0)
    output["temporal_hinge_active_ratio"] = (hinge > 0).float().mean()
    output["temporal_margin_mean"] = margin.mean()
    output["temporal_margin_std"] = margin.std(unbiased=False)
    margin_flat = margin.detach().float().flatten()
    output["temporal_margin_p10"] = torch.quantile(margin_flat, 0.10)
    output["temporal_margin_p50"] = torch.quantile(margin_flat, 0.50)
    output["temporal_margin_p90"] = torch.quantile(margin_flat, 0.90)

    if hinge_cfg.squared:
        hinge = hinge.square()
    return hinge.mean()


def swm_forward(self, batch, stage, cfg):
    """Encode observations, predict next states, compute spherical losses.

    Losses:
      pred_loss   — configurable prediction loss in raw or normalized space
      reg_loss    — configurable anti-collapse loss in raw or normalized space
      temporal_hinge_loss — optional hinge loss on consecutive latent pairs
      loss        — pred_loss + λ_reg * reg_loss + λ_hinge * temporal_hinge_loss

    Note:
      temporal_straightness is logged as a monitoring metric using Euclidean
      displacement vectors between consecutive unit-norm latents. On the sphere
      this is only a chord-space approximation, so it may not reflect true
      geodesic straightening accurately.
    """
    ctx_len = cfg.wm.history_size
    n_preds = cfg.wm.num_preds
    reg_type = cfg.loss.regularizer.type
    reg_lambd = cfg.loss.regularizer.weight
    hinge_cfg = cfg.loss.temporal_hinge
    reg_space = cfg.loss.regularizer.get("space", "normalized")
    pred_space = cfg.loss.pred.get("space", "normalized")
    context_space = cfg.loss.pred.get("context_space", pred_space)

    # Replace NaN values with 0 (occurs at sequence boundaries)
    batch["action"] = torch.nan_to_num(batch["action"], 0.0)

    output = self.model.encode(batch)

    emb = output["emb"]  # (B, T, D), unit vectors
    act_emb = output["act_emb"]

    # Make training-time autoregressive context explicit so raw-consistent
    # experiments can feed predictor history from the same space used for the
    # prediction target and planning rollout.
    ctx_emb = get_context_tensor(output, space=context_space)[:, :ctx_len]
    ctx_act = act_emb[:, :ctx_len]

    pred_raw = self.model.predict_raw(ctx_emb, ctx_act)
    pred_emb = self.model.normalize_embeddings(pred_raw)

    pred_source, tgt_source = get_loss_space_tensors(
        output,
        pred_raw=pred_raw,
        pred_norm=pred_emb,
        n_preds=n_preds,
        space=pred_space,
    )
    output["pred_loss"] = compute_pred_loss(pred_source, tgt_source, cfg)
    output["rollout_loss"] = compute_multistep_rollout_loss(
        output,
        model=self.model,
        cfg=cfg,
    )

    reg_emb = get_regularizer_tensor(output, space=reg_space)
    reg_scope = cfg.loss.regularizer.get("scope", "full").lower()
    if reg_scope == "pred_window":
        reg_emb = reg_emb[:, : ctx_len + n_preds]
    elif reg_scope != "full":
        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported loss.regularizer.scope: {reg_scope}")
    if reg_type == "spread":
        output["spread_loss"] = spread_loss(reg_emb, cfg.loss.spread.margin)
        output["reg_loss"] = output["spread_loss"]
    elif reg_type == "uniformity":
        output["uniformity_loss"] = uniformity_loss(
            reg_emb,
            cfg.loss.uniformity.t,
            mode=cfg.loss.uniformity.get("mode", "all_pairs"),
            temporal_exclusion=cfg.loss.uniformity.get("temporal_exclusion", 0),
        )
        output["reg_loss"] = output["uniformity_loss"]
    elif reg_type == "infonce":
        tgt_emb = output["emb"][:, n_preds:]
        output["infonce_loss"] = infonce_loss(
            pred_emb, tgt_emb, cfg.loss.infonce.temperature
        )
        output["reg_loss"] = output["infonce_loss"]
    else:
        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported loss.regularizer.type: {reg_type}")

    output["temporal_hinge_loss"] = compute_temporal_hinge(
        output,
        model=self.model,
        cfg=cfg,
    )

    output["loss"] = (
        output["pred_loss"]
        + reg_lambd * output["reg_loss"]
        + hinge_cfg.weight * output["temporal_hinge_loss"]
    )
    if output["rollout_loss"] is not None:
        rollout_weight = cfg.loss.rollout.get("weight", 0.0)
        output["loss"] = output["loss"] + rollout_weight * output["rollout_loss"]
    # Approximate monitor only: on spherical latents this uses chord-space
    # displacements, not a geodesic straightness measure on the manifold.
    output["temporal_straightness"] = temporal_straightness(emb)

    losses_dict = {
        f"{stage}/{k}": v.detach()
        for k, v in output.items()
        if "loss" in k and torch.is_tensor(v)
    }
    metrics_dict = {
        f"{stage}/{k}": v.detach()
        for k, v in output.items()
        if (
            torch.is_tensor(v)
            and (
                k == "temporal_straightness"
                or k == "temporal_hinge_active_ratio"
                or k.startswith("temporal_margin_")
            )
        )
    }
    self.log_dict({**losses_dict, **metrics_dict}, on_step=True, sync_dist=True)
    return output


@hydra.main(version_base=None, config_path="./config/train", config_name="swm")
def run(cfg):
    #########################
    ##       dataset       ##
    #########################

    dataset = swm.data.HDF5Dataset(**cfg.data.dataset, transform=None)
    transforms = [
        get_img_preprocessor(source="pixels", target="pixels", img_size=cfg.img_size)
    ]

    with open_dict(cfg):
        for col in cfg.data.dataset.keys_to_load:
            if col.startswith("pixels"):
                continue

            normalizer = get_column_normalizer(dataset, col, col)
            transforms.append(normalizer)

            setattr(cfg.wm, f"{col}_dim", dataset.get_dim(col))

    transform = spt.data.transforms.Compose(*transforms)
    dataset.transform = transform

    rnd_gen = torch.Generator().manual_seed(cfg.seed)
    train_set, val_set = spt.data.random_split(
        dataset, lengths=[cfg.train_split, 1 - cfg.train_split], generator=rnd_gen
    )

    train = torch.utils.data.DataLoader(
        train_set, **cfg.loader, shuffle=True, drop_last=True, generator=rnd_gen
    )
    val = torch.utils.data.DataLoader(
        val_set, **cfg.loader, shuffle=False, drop_last=False
    )

    ##############################
    ##       model / optim      ##
    ##############################

    encoder = spt.backbone.utils.vit_hf(
        cfg.encoder_scale,
        patch_size=cfg.patch_size,
        image_size=cfg.img_size,
        pretrained=False,
        use_mask_token=False,
    )

    hidden_dim = encoder.config.hidden_size
    embed_dim = cfg.wm.get("embed_dim", hidden_dim)
    effective_act_dim = cfg.data.dataset.frameskip * cfg.wm.action_dim

    predictor = ARPredictor(
        num_frames=cfg.wm.history_size,
        input_dim=embed_dim,
        hidden_dim=hidden_dim,
        output_dim=hidden_dim,
        **cfg.predictor,
    )

    action_encoder = Embedder(input_dim=effective_act_dim, emb_dim=embed_dim)

    head_cfg = cfg.encoder.projection_head
    projector = build_projection_head(hidden_dim, embed_dim, head_cfg)
    pred_proj = build_projection_head(hidden_dim, embed_dim, head_cfg)
    inference_cfg = cfg.wm.get("inference", {})
    pred_cfg = cfg.loss.get("pred", {})
    training_context_space = pred_cfg.get(
        "context_space", pred_cfg.get("space", "normalized")
    )

    world_model = SphericalJEPA(
        encoder=encoder,
        predictor=predictor,
        action_encoder=action_encoder,
        projector=projector,
        pred_proj=pred_proj,
        inference_rollout_state_space=inference_cfg.get(
            "rollout_state_space", "normalized"
        ),
        inference_cost_space=inference_cfg.get("cost_space", "normalized"),
        inference_cost_type=inference_cfg.get("cost_type", "cosine"),
        analysis_prediction_space=cfg.loss.pred.get("space", "normalized"),
        training_context_space=training_context_space,
    )
    if cfg.loss.temporal_hinge.get("dynamic", {}).get("enabled", False):
        world_model.dynamic_margin_head = nn.Linear(2 * embed_dim, 1)
        nn.init.zeros_(world_model.dynamic_margin_head.weight)
        nn.init.zeros_(world_model.dynamic_margin_head.bias)

    optimizers = {
        "model_opt": {
            "modules": "model",
            "optimizer": dict(cfg.optimizer),
            "scheduler": {"type": "LinearWarmupCosineAnnealingLR"},
            "interval": "epoch",
        },
    }

    data_module = spt.data.DataModule(train=train, val=val)
    world_model = spt.Module(
        model=world_model,
        forward=partial(swm_forward, cfg=cfg),
        optim=optimizers,
    )

    ##########################
    ##       training       ##
    ##########################

    run_id = cfg.get("subdir") or ""
    run_dir = Path(swm.data.utils.get_cache_dir(), run_id)

    logger = None
    backend = cfg.get("logger_backend", "swanlab")
    if backend == "swanlab" and cfg.swanlab.enabled:
        if SwanLabLogger is None:
            raise ImportError("swanlab is not installed. Run: pip install swanlab")
        logger = SwanLabLogger(**cfg.swanlab.config)
        logger.log_hyperparams(OmegaConf.to_container(cfg))
    elif backend == "wandb" and cfg.wandb.enabled:
        logger = WandbLogger(**cfg.wandb.config)
        logger.log_hyperparams(OmegaConf.to_container(cfg))

    run_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    with open(run_dir / "config.yaml", "w") as f:
        OmegaConf.save(cfg, f)

    object_dump_callback = ModelObjectCallBack(
        dirpath=run_dir,
        filename=cfg.output_model_name,
        epoch_interval=1,
    )

    trainer = pl.Trainer(
        **cfg.trainer,
        callbacks=[object_dump_callback],
        num_sanity_val_steps=1,
        logger=logger,
        enable_checkpointing=True,
    )

    manager = spt.Manager(
        trainer=trainer,
        module=world_model,
        data=data_module,
        ckpt_path=run_dir / f"{cfg.output_model_name}_weights.ckpt",
```

```
Output:

Why this matters:
- Planning quality depends on repeated rollout, not only one-step accuracy.
- The current SWM gains already suggest the representation is good enough to
  make dynamics the next likely bottleneck.
- A small-horizon multi-step loss is the most direct way to align training with
  MPC usage.

Main caveat:
- start with a small weight and short horizon; large-horizon rollout losses can
  destabilize optimization and hurt the strong one-step predictor.

### Priority 4: add a lightweight inverse-dynamics auxiliary loss

Recommended direction:
- predict action from `(z_t, z_{t+1})` or from `(context_t, z_{t+1})`
- use it as an auxiliary loss with a modest weight, not as a primary objective

Why this matters:
- It can encourage the latent space to retain action-relevant information,
  which is especially useful for PushT, Cube, and Reacher.
- It is more likely to help in continuous-control settings where the same visual
  state can support multiple future outcomes depending on action.

Main caveat:
- inverse dynamics is ill-posed when multiple actions induce near-identical
  transitions, so it should remain an auxiliary bias rather than the main
  training signal.

### Priority 5: combine additions only after isolated tests

Recommended sequence:
1. baseline best SWM config with more seeds
2. best SWM + transition-aware continuity
3. best SWM + short-horizon multi-step loss
4. best SWM + inverse-dynamics auxiliary
```

```
+def transition_pair_features(
+    z_t: torch.Tensor,
+    z_tp1: torch.Tensor,
+    *,
+    detach: bool = False,
+) -> torch.Tensor:
+    """Concatenate consecutive latent pairs for transition-level heads."""
+    if z_t.shape != z_tp1.shape:
+        raise ValueError(
+            f"z_t and z_tp1 must have the same shape, got {z_t.shape} vs {z_tp1.shape}"
+        )
+    if detach:
+        z_t = z_t.detach()
+        z_tp1 = z_tp1.detach()
+    return torch.cat([z_t, z_tp1], dim=-1)
+
+
+def inverse_dynamics_loss(
+    z_t: torch.Tensor,
+    z_tp1: torch.Tensor,
+    action: torch.Tensor,
+    head: nn.Module,
+    *,
+    detach_input: bool = False,
+) -> torch.Tensor:
+    """Predict the action that connects consecutive latent states."""
+    if z_t.numel() == 0:
+        return z_t.new_tensor(0.0)
+    pred_action = head(
+        transition_pair_features(z_t, z_tp1, detach=detach_input)
+    )
+    if pred_action.shape != action.shape:
+        raise ValueError(
+            "inverse dynamics prediction and action target must have the same "
+            f"shape, got {pred_action.shape} vs {action.shape}"
+        )
+    return F.mse_loss(pred_action, action.float())
+
+
+def transition_distance_prediction_loss(
+    z_t: torch.Tensor,
+    z_tp1: torch.Tensor,
+    head: nn.Module,
+    *,
+    metric: str,
+    detach_input: bool = True,
+) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:
+    """Predict consecutive-state latent distance as a diagnostic auxiliary."""
+    if z_t.numel() == 0:
+        empty = z_t.new_empty(z_t.shape[:-1])
+        return z_t.new_tensor(0.0), empty, empty
+
+    pred_dist = F.softplus(
+        head(transition_pair_features(z_t, z_tp1, detach=detach_input)).squeeze(-1)
+    )
+
+    if metric == "l2":
+        target_dist = torch.linalg.vector_norm(z_tp1 - z_t, dim=-1)
+    elif metric == "cosine":
+        z_t_norm = F.normalize(z_t, dim=-1, eps=1e-8)
+        z_tp1_norm = F.normalize(z_tp1, dim=-1, eps=1e-8)
+        target_dist = 1.0 - (z_t_norm * z_tp1_norm).sum(dim=-1)
+    else:
+        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported transition distance metric: {metric}")
+
+    loss = F.smooth_l1_loss(pred_dist, target_dist.detach())
+    return loss, pred_dist, target_dist
+
+
```

```
    batch["action"] = torch.nan_to_num(batch["action"], 0.0)

    output = self.model.encode(batch)

    emb = output["emb"]  # (B, T, D)
    act_emb = output["act_emb"]

    ctx_emb = emb[:, :ctx_len]
    ctx_act = act_emb[:, :ctx_len]

    tgt_emb = emb[:, n_preds:]  # label
    pred_emb = self.model.predict(ctx_emb, ctx_act)  # pred

    # LeWM loss
    output["pred_loss"] = (pred_emb - tgt_emb).pow(2).mean()
    output["sigreg_loss"] = self.sigreg(emb.transpose(0, 1))
    output["temporal_hinge_loss"] = compute_temporal_hinge(
        output, model=self.model, cfg=cfg
    )
    inverse_cfg = cfg.loss.get("inverse_dynamics", {})
    inverse_enabled = (
        inverse_cfg.get("enabled", False)
        or inverse_cfg.get("weight", 0.0) > 0.0
    )
    if inverse_enabled:
        if not hasattr(self.model, "inverse_dynamics_head"):
            raise AttributeError(
                "loss.inverse_dynamics requires model.inverse_dynamics_head"
            )
        output["inverse_dynamics_loss"] = inverse_dynamics_loss(
            emb[:, :-1],
            emb[:, 1:],
            output["action"][:, :-1],
            self.model.inverse_dynamics_head,
            detach_input=inverse_cfg.get("detach_input", False),
        )

    dist_cfg = cfg.loss.get("transition_distance", {})
    dist_enabled = (
        dist_cfg.get("enabled", False)
        or dist_cfg.get("weight", 0.0) > 0.0
    )
    if dist_enabled:
        if not hasattr(self.model, "transition_distance_head"):
            raise AttributeError(
                "loss.transition_distance requires model.transition_distance_head"
            )
        (
            output["transition_distance_loss"],
            pred_dist,
            target_dist,
        ) = transition_distance_prediction_loss(
            emb[:, :-1],
            emb[:, 1:],
            self.model.transition_distance_head,
            metric=dist_cfg.get("metric", "l2"),
            detach_input=dist_cfg.get("detach_input", True),
        )
        output["transition_distance_pred_mean"] = pred_dist.mean()
        output["transition_distance_target_mean"] = target_dist.mean()
        output["transition_distance_target_std"] = target_dist.std(unbiased=False)

    output["loss"] = (
        output["pred_loss"]
        + sigreg_lambd * output["sigreg_loss"]
        + hinge_cfg.weight * output["temporal_hinge_loss"]
    )
    if "inverse_dynamics_loss" in output:
        output["loss"] = (
            output["loss"]
            + inverse_cfg.get("weight", 0.0) * output["inverse_dynamics_loss"]
        )
    if "transition_distance_loss" in output:
        output["loss"] = (
            output["loss"]
            + dist_cfg.get("weight", 0.0) * output["transition_distance_loss"]
        )
    output["temporal_straightness"] = temporal_straightness(emb)

    losses_dict = {f"{stage}/{k}": v.detach() for k, v in output.items() if "loss" in k}
    metrics_dict = {
        f"{stage}/{k}": v.detach()
        for k, v in output.items()
        if (
            torch.is_tensor(v)
            and (
                k == "temporal_straightness"
                or k == "temporal_hinge_active_ratio"
                or k.startswith("temporal_margin_")
                or k.startswith("transition_distance_")
            )
        )
    }
    self.log_dict({**losses_dict, **metrics_dict}, on_step=True, sync_dist=True)
    return output
```

### User - 2026-04-27 05:54:25 EDT

detach_input作用是什么，建议怎么用

### Assistant - 2026-04-27 05:54:36 EDT

````
`detach_input` 控制 auxiliary head 的梯度要不要反传回 latent encoder / projector。

以 inverse dynamics 为例：

```python
loss = MSE(head(z_t, z_tp1), action)
```

如果：

```yaml
detach_input: False
```

梯度路径是：

```text
inverse_dynamics_loss -> head
                      -> z_t, z_tp1
                      -> encoder/projector
```

也就是说它不只训练 action-prediction head，还会塑形 latent，让 latent 更保留 action-relevant 信息。

如果：

```yaml
detach_input: True
```

梯度路径是：

```text
inverse_dynamics_loss -> head only
```

`z_t, z_tp1` 被 stop-gradient，auxiliary 只变成诊断 head，不改变 representation。

我的建议：

**inverse_dynamics**

优先用：

```yaml
loss.inverse_dynamics.weight=0.01
loss.inverse_dynamics.detach_input=False
```

因为我们加它的目的就是让 latent 更 action-aware。它应该反传到 representation。  
如果发现主任务 eval 明显掉、`pred_loss/reg_loss` 被扰动很大，再试：

```yaml
loss.inverse_dynamics.weight=0.001
```

或者临时设 `detach_input=True` 做诊断，看看 action 是否可从当前 latent transition 中被读出来。

**transition_distance**

优先用：

```yaml
loss.transition_distance.weight=0.01
loss.transition_distance.detach_input=True
```

因为它目前更像 diagnostic：判断 `(z_t,z_{t+1})` 的 transition distance 是否可预测。先不要让它塑形 latent，否则可能又回到 hinge_dynamic 那类 “为了距离目标改坏 geometry” 的问题。

只有当你确认它的预测有意义，并且想让 latent transition scale 更可读时，再试：

```yaml
loss.transition_distance.detach_input=False
loss.transition_distance.weight=0.001
```

简化成一句：

```text
inverse_dynamics: 默认 detach_input=False
transition_distance: 默认 detach_input=True
```

这也正是现在配置里的默认值。
````

### User - 2026-04-27 06:05:17 EDT

```
先做 inverse dynamics auxiliary
  再做 dist prediction diagnostic
  最后做 temporal distance head   你前面建议了三个，但代码里只增加了两个配置
```

### Assistant - 2026-04-27 06:05:26 EDT

````
对，当前代码只加了前两个，这是有意的最小改动，不是漏掉了。

我当时的排序是：

```text
1. inverse dynamics auxiliary
2. dist prediction diagnostic
3. temporal distance head
```

但实现时先落了：

```text
1. inverse_dynamics
2. transition_distance
```

原因是第三个 `temporal distance head` 不是一个很小的 patch。它需要额外决定：

```text
采样哪些 (i, j)
预测 gap 的形式：classification / regression / ranking
是否只同轨迹采样
max_gap 怎么设
是否对 batch 内不同 trajectory 加 negative
loss 权重和 logging 怎么设计
```

如果直接一起塞进去，会让这次改动从 “两个 transition-level auxiliary” 变成一个新 representation objective，实验解释会变脏。

我建议现在按这个顺序跑：

```text
A. SWM + inverse_dynamics
B. SWM + transition_distance diagnostic
C. 如果 A/B 有信号，再加 temporal_distance_head
```

第三个我建议单独做一版更干净，例如：

```yaml
loss.temporal_distance:
  weight: 0.0
  hidden_dim: ${wm.embed_dim}
  max_gap: 3
  space: normalized
  detach_input: False
```

初版可以做同一 trajectory 内 gap classification：

```text
input:  [z_i, z_j]
target: clamp(j - i, max_gap)
loss:   cross_entropy
```

但我不建议现在马上和前两个一起提交，除非你确定要直接跑这一组。当前两个配置已经足够开始第一轮实验。
````

### User - 2026-04-27 06:09:27 EDT

那先等一等我前面两个结果，对于swm上建议我使用什么配置上增加这两个新增的方案

### Assistant - 2026-04-27 06:09:40 EDT

````
建议先只在当前最稳的 SWM baseline 上加，不要和 hinge / rollout 混跑：

```text
swm_mlp_bn_uniform_w02_t2_temporal_masked_2_dim64
```

也就是保持：

```yaml
loss.regularizer.weight=0.2
loss.uniformity.t=2
loss.uniformity.mode=temporal_masked
loss.uniformity.temporal_exclusion=2
wm.embed_dim=64
loss.temporal_hinge.weight=0
loss.rollout.weight=0
```

先跑两组。

**1. inverse dynamics 主实验**

优先配置：

```bash
loss.inverse_dynamics.weight=0.01
loss.inverse_dynamics.detach_input=False
loss.inverse_dynamics.space=normalized
```

如果担心扰动太强，可以同时跑一个弱版：

```bash
loss.inverse_dynamics.weight=0.001
loss.inverse_dynamics.detach_input=False
loss.inverse_dynamics.space=normalized
```

我会把 `0.01` 作为首选，因为 action MSE 通常需要有一点权重才会对 representation 起作用，但不会像 hinge 那样直接夹 latent 距离。

**2. transition distance diagnostic**

先用 diagnostic，不塑形 latent：

```bash
loss.transition_distance.weight=0.01
loss.transition_distance.detach_input=True
loss.transition_distance.space=normalized
loss.transition_distance.metric=cosine
```

这组主要看：

```text
transition_distance_loss
transition_distance_pred_mean
transition_distance_target_mean/std
```

它如果 eval 没变化很正常，因为 `detach_input=True` 时主要训练 head，不改变 encoder。它的价值是判断 transition scale 是否能被当前 latent pair 读出来。

如果 diagnostic 看起来能学到，再单独试一个很弱的塑形版：

```bash
loss.transition_distance.weight=0.001
loss.transition_distance.detach_input=False
loss.transition_distance.space=normalized
loss.transition_distance.metric=cosine
```

**不建议现在这样混：**

```bash
loss.inverse_dynamics.weight>0 loss.transition_distance.weight>0
```

先分开跑，否则很难解释是哪一个带来的变化。

所以第一批我建议四个 run：

```text
baseline rerun
inverse_w001
inverse_w01
distdiag_w01_detach
```

如果资源有限，就先跑：

```text
inverse_w01
distdiag_w01_detach
```
````

### User - 2026-04-27 06:11:01 EDT

在temporal mask上增加新增的方案配置确定不会有问题吧，不用吧mask这个选项去掉吗

### Assistant - 2026-04-27 06:11:10 EDT

````
我建议**先不要去掉 temporal_mask**，直接在当前 `temporal_masked_2` baseline 上加新增方案。

理由是这两个新增 auxiliary 和 hinge 不一样：

```text
temporal_mask:
  只是让 uniformity 不推开近邻时间步

inverse_dynamics:
  让 z_t, z_{t+1} 保留能预测 action 的信息

transition_distance diagnostic:
  读出当前 latent transition distance，可 detach，不塑形 latent
```

它们不是同一种约束。`temporal_mask` 是在 anti-collapse regularizer 里改 negative pair 选择；`inverse_dynamics` 是 action-aware 辅助监督。二者不会像 `temporal_mask + hinge` 那样同时在“近邻 pair 该近还是远”上直接拉扯。

所以第一轮建议保持：

```bash
loss.uniformity.mode=temporal_masked
loss.uniformity.temporal_exclusion=2
```

然后只加：

```bash
loss.inverse_dynamics.weight=0.01
loss.inverse_dynamics.detach_input=False
loss.inverse_dynamics.space=normalized
```

或者 diagnostic：

```bash
loss.transition_distance.weight=0.01
loss.transition_distance.detach_input=True
loss.transition_distance.space=normalized
loss.transition_distance.metric=cosine
```

什么时候需要去掉 mask？只有一种情况：如果 inverse dynamics 在 `temporal_masked_2` 上无收益甚至负向，但你怀疑 mask 已经把局部时间结构“保护”得太强，导致 auxiliary 没有边际空间。那第二轮再做对照：

```text
uniformity all_pairs + inverse_dynamics
temporal_masked_2 + inverse_dynamics
```

但不建议第一轮就去掉。当前最合理的实验设计是：**在最强 baseline 上只改变一个因素**。这里最强 baseline 就是 `SWM + temporal_masked_2`。
````

### Assistant - 2026-04-27 21:25:26 EDT

````
是的，SWM baseline 上加这些 temporal/rollout/hinge 类约束，整体看是**负向收益明显**。你这批结果已经足够支持一个结论：**当前 SWM 的强项主要来自 `uniformity + temporal_masked_2` 的表征几何；继续往它上面加“局部时序约束”大多会破坏这个几何。**

先看 SWM 2026-04-25 baseline：

| Task | SWM baseline | rollout | fixed hinge best | dynamic hinge |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| TwoRoom | 91.0 | 89.6 | 88.6 | **71.2** |
| Cube | 73.2 | 70.0 | 73.8 | 71.8 |
| PushT | 82.4 | 78.6 | 81.0 | 82.0 |
| Reacher | 58.4 | 57.8 | 60.0 | 57.2 |

SWM dynamic hinge 平均：

```text
baseline avg = (91.0 + 73.2 + 82.4 + 58.4) / 4 = 76.25
dynamic avg  = (71.2 + 71.8 + 82.0 + 57.2) / 4 = 70.55
delta        = -5.70
```

主要负向来自 TwoRoom：

```text
TwoRoom: 91.0 -> 71.2  (-19.8)
```

这不是小波动，是结构性破坏。Cube/PushT/Reacher 虽然没有大崩，但也没有稳定收益。

**LeWM 对照**

LeWM dynamic hinge：

| Task | LeWM baseline | LeWM dynamic |
|---|---:|---:|
| TwoRoom | 93.0 | 100.0 |
| PushT | 83.6 | 19.0 |
| Reacher | 58.8 | 58.2 |

这个模式也很清楚：

- TwoRoom 喜欢 weak/loose continuity，dynamic 退化成 fixed `m≈0.5` 后也能涨到 100。
- PushT 极度反对 continuity，直接从 83.6 掉到 19。
- Reacher 基本无收益。
- Cube fixed m05 只有 70.2 vs 70.0，几乎不动；dynamic 没给 Cube 数据，但大概率也不会是主收益点。

所以 dynamic hinge 不是“在 LeWM 有效、SWM 无效”，而是：**它只在 TwoRoom 这种低维、平滑、拓扑简单的任务上可能碰巧有效；对 manipulation/contact 更危险。**

**为什么 SWM 上负向更明显**

SWM 的 baseline 已经用了：

```text
normalized spherical latent
BN projector
uniformity w=0.2, t=2
temporal_masked_2
dim64
```

这套东西本质上已经在平衡：

```text
全局分散性
局部时间邻居不过度排斥
规划用的 cosine geometry
```

而 hinge / dynamic hinge / rollout 都会额外改变局部 transition geometry。问题是 MPC 不只是要 `z_t -> z_{t+1}` 平滑，还要 goal distance 在 latent 里可用。你加的这些项可能让 one-step 或局部连续性更“漂亮”，但损害了最终 planning metric。

尤其 TwoRoom 掉 19.8，很可能是 dynamic hinge 把房间/门口/可达性边界附近的 latent 结构压平了。TwoRoom planning 很依赖 goal distance 的拓扑分辨率，局部 continuity 一强，反而把关键边界变钝。

**对 rollout 的判断**

rollout `w=0.1,s=2` 也全负：

```text
TwoRoom: 91.0 -> 89.6
Cube:    73.2 -> 70.0
PushT:   82.4 -> 78.6
Reacher: 58.4 -> 57.8
```

这说明当前 SWM 的短 rollout auxiliary 也没有提供帮助。可能原因是 weight 太大，也可能是 autoregressive training 和 planning cost 的空间/误差传播方式还没对齐。但至少 `w=0.1` 不应该继续作为默认方向。

**下一步建议**

1. **停止 hinge_dynamic 和 fixed hinge 的主线 sweep**

尤其不要再在 SWM baseline 上调 `base_margin/min/max`。当前证据已经很差。

2. **新增 inverse dynamics 仍然值得测**

它和 hinge 不同，不是强迫相邻状态近，而是让 latent transition 保留 action 信息。建议第一批只跑：

```bash
loss.inverse_dynamics.weight=0.01
loss.inverse_dynamics.detach_input=False
loss.inverse_dynamics.space=normalized
loss.temporal_hinge.weight=0
loss.rollout.weight=0
```

如果负向，再降：

```bash
loss.inverse_dynamics.weight=0.001
```

3. **transition_distance 只当 diagnostic**

先不要期待 eval 提升：

```bash
loss.transition_distance.weight=0.01
loss.transition_distance.detach_input=True
```

它主要回答 “当前 latent transition distance 是否可读”。如果 detach=True，eval 理论上不应大变。

4. **如果 inverse dynamics 也负向，就别再给 SWM 加局部 auxiliary**

那就说明当前 SWM geometry 已经很脆，下一步应该转向：
- 多 seed 确认 baseline
- 改 planner/cost/rollout inference
- 或做 value/reward/goal-conditioned objective，而不是继续加局部 latent 正则

我的当前判断是：

```text
SWM + temporal_masked_2 是主线 baseline。
hinge / dynamic hinge / rollout_w0.1 都不是主线。
下一步唯一值得测的新增训练项是 inverse_dynamics，且必须单独测。
```
````

````
用你这批最新 rerun baseline 算：

| Model | TwoRoom | Cube | PushT | Reacher | Avg |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| LeWM latest | 93.0 | 70.0 | 83.6 | 58.8 | 76.35 |
| LeWM + inverse w001 | 93.6 | 67.8 | 84.6 | 60.0 | 76.50 |
| LeWM + transition_distance w001 | 95.0 | 71.0 | 84.6 | 56.2 | 76.70 |
| SWM latest | 91.0 | 73.2 | 82.4 | 58.4 | 76.25 |
| SWM + inverse w001 | 58.0 | 72.0 | 75.8 | 57.8 | 65.90 |
| SWM + transition_distance w001 | 78.2 | 72.6 | 78.8 | 59.2 | 72.20 |

**关键结论**

LeWM 上两个新增项都不是大突破，但有轻微正信号：

```text

transition_distance: +0.35 avg
```

分任务看，LeWM + inverse 更稳一点：

```text

Cube -2.2
PushT +1.0
Reacher +1.2

LeWM + transition_distance 则更分裂：

```text
TwoRoom +2.0
Cube +1.0

Reacher -2.6
```

所以 LeWM 可以继续小范围确认，尤其 inverse dynamics。

SWM 上结果很差：

```text
inverse: 76.25 -> 65.90  (-10.35)
transition_distance: 76.25 -> 72.20  (-4.05)
```

最异常的是 TwoRoom：

```text
SWM inverse: 91.0 -> 58.0
SWM transition_distance: 91.0 -> 78.2
```

这说明 SWM 的 `uniformity + temporal_masked_2` geometry 很脆。你给它加 transition-level objective，即使很弱 `w=0.001`，也会破坏规划用的 latent topology。尤其 TwoRoom，应该非常依赖 goal-distance / room-door topology；auxiliary 改动把这个结构打坏了。

一个值得特别注意的点：如果 `transition_distance` 用的是默认 `detach_input=True`，理论上它不应该反传改 encoder，只训练 head。但 SWM 仍然掉很多，说明要核查两件事：

1. 你实际 run 里是否覆盖了 `loss.transition_distance.detach_input=False`
2. 代码里 target distance 的统计/logging 是否没有无意中影响主 loss

按当前代码，`detach_input=True` 时 target 也 detach，encoder 不应收到 transition distance 梯度。如果配置确实是默认 detach=True，而 eval 仍然大幅变差，那更像是实验方差、run 命名/配置不一致，或者训练时实际用了非默认覆盖。这个需要看保存的 `config.yaml` 确认。

**我建议下一步**

1. **SWM 暂停新增 auxiliary 主线**

不要继续在 SWM `temporal_masked_2` baseline 上 sweep inverse/transition。当前信号太差。

loss:

    detach_input: true
```

如果确实是 `true`，这个 78.2/78.8 的下降不太符合代码预期，需要复跑确认。  
如果是 `false`，那结果合理：它在塑形 latent，并且负向。

inverse_dynamics_w001
inverse_dynamics_w003
```

别急着上 `w01`，可能太强。

4. **SWM 若还要测，先只做 detach 诊断复跑**

```bash

loss.transition_distance.detach_input=True
```

这组如果复跑仍大幅掉分，就不要把它当 training objective，先排查配置/随机性。

当前判断：
````

````
这个 config 是 **LeWM 的 transition_distance**，不是 SWM。因为里面有：

```yaml

  sigreg:
```

SWM 配置不会有 `sigreg`，而是 `regularizer/uniformity` 那套。

所以这个 config 对应的大概率是：

```text
*_lewm_transition_distance_w001
```

而且这里其实是：

```yaml
transition_distance.weight: 0.01
```

不是 `w001=0.001`。你的 run name 叫 `w001`，但保存 config 是 `0.01`，命名和真实配置不一致。这个要特别注意，否则后面会误判 weight scale。

对这个 LeWM config 来说：

```yaml
detach_input: true
```

含义是 transition distance loss **不会反向改 encoder/projector latent**，只训练 `transition_distance_head`。所以它对 LeWM eval 的影响理论上应该很小，主要来自：

1. 训练中新增 head 的 optimizer 参数稍微改变了优化器参数组/调度细节；
2. 随机性；
3. 额外 loss/head 可能影响 Lightning/module 状态保存，但不应改变主模型梯度；
4. 如果代码里有 bug，否则不会塑形 representation。

因此 LeWM transition distance 的 `95/71/84.6/56.2` 不应该被解读为 “transition_distance 改善了 representation”。它更像 diagnostic head + run variance。尤其 Reacher 掉 2.6，也符合这种噪声/训练扰动。

你需要再看 SWM 那个保存 config，重点确认：

```yaml

  regularizer:
  uniformity:
  transition_distance:
    weight: ?
    detach_input: ?
    space: ?
    metric: ?
```

如果 SWM 也是：

```yaml
detach_input: true
```

那 SWM transition_distance 大幅掉分就不太合理，优先怀疑 run variance、命名/配置混乱，或实际评估 checkpoint 不对应这个 config。

另外，当前命名建议统一：

```text
w001 = 0.001
w01  = 0.01
w1   = 0.1
```

你这个 config 应该叫：

```text
lewm_transition_distance_w01
```

不是 `w001`。这点最好先修正实验记录，否则后面分析会越来越乱。
````

### Assistant - 2026-04-27 21:45:23 EDT

````
这个 SWM config 确认了两件重要的事：

1. `transition_distance.detach_input: true`
2. `transition_distance.weight: 0.01`，也就是你的 `w001` 命名约定里实际代表 `0.01`

在当前代码语义下，`detach_input: true` 时：

```text
transition_distance_loss -> transition_distance_head
                         X encoder/projector/predictor latent
```

所以它**不应该通过梯度直接改变 SWM 的 representation**。它只会训练一个额外 head，主模型的梯度不应包含这项。

因此，SWM transition_distance 结果：

```text
TwoRoom: 91.0 -> 78.2
Cube:    73.2 -> 72.6
PushT:   82.4 -> 78.8
Reacher: 58.4 -> 59.2
Avg:     76.25 -> 72.20
```

不能直接解释为 “transition distance objective 把 latent 几何训坏了”。如果 `detach_input=true` 确实生效，更合理的解释是：

**1. 这组不是纯训练目标影响，而是 run variance / 配置差异**

你这里还有一个差异：

```yaml
regularizer.scope: pred_window
rollout.steps: 2
```

之前主 baseline 名字是 `temporal_masked_2_dim64`，但不确定它是不是同样：

```yaml
regularizer.scope: pred_window
```

如果 baseline 是 `scope: full`，那这个对比不干净。`pred_window` 会让 uniformity 只作用在 `ctx_len + n_preds`，不是完整序列，可能影响几何。

`rollout.steps=2` 在 `rollout.weight=0` 时按代码不会启用 rollout loss，但 dataset `num_steps` 会受它影响：

```yaml
num_steps: max(num_preds, rollout.steps) + history_size
```

所以即使 `rollout.weight=0`，`rollout.steps=2` 也可能让训练序列长度变长，从而影响 uniformity pair pool / temporal mask 行为。这个也会污染对比。

**2. 额外 head 参数本身不应影响主模型，但可能改变随机初始化顺序**

如果 head 在 model 里创建，即使不反传到 encoder，也会：
- 增加 optimizer 参数；
- 改变后续随机数消耗；
- 可能改变训练非确定性路径。

通常不该导致 TwoRoom -12.8，但在单 seed + GPU nondeterminism 下不能排除。

**3. 需要做一个 zero-weight / detached-head sanity check**

为了确认是不是 auxiliary 本身导致，建议跑一个非常关键的对照：

```bash
loss.transition_distance.weight=0.0
loss.rollout.steps=2
loss.regularizer.scope=pred_window
```

这等价于 “和 transition_distance run 保持其余配置一致，但不创建/训练 head”。

如果这个也低，那问题不是 transition_distance，而是 `scope/num_steps/seed`。  
如果这个回到 91/82/58 附近，再说明额外 head/随机性/训练流程有影响。

**下一步我建议这样排**

先别继续分析 transition_distance 的收益，先做对照清洗：

```text
A. SWM exact config, transition_distance.weight=0.0
B. SWM exact config, transition_distance.weight=0.01, detach_input=true
C. SWM exact config, transition_distance.weight=0.01, detach_input=false
```

A 和 B 的差异理论上应该很小。  
B 和 C 才是 “是否让 transition distance 塑形 latent” 的比较。

另外要确认 baseline 和新增实验完全一致：

```text
regularizer.scope
rollout.steps
num_steps
seed
embed_dim
uniformity mode/exclusion
```

当前这份 config 里最需要警惕的是：

```yaml
regularizer.scope: pred_window
rollout.steps: 2
```

这两个可能让它不是你以为的那个 SWM baseline。
````

### User - 2026-04-27 21:45:44 EDT

rollout.steps: 2但rollout weight=0没问题

### Assistant - 2026-04-27 21:46:05 EDT

````
`rollout.weight=0` 确实表示 **rollout loss 没有进总 loss**，这点没问题。

但在这个 repo 里，`rollout.steps=2` 不是完全 no-op，因为 data config 里 `num_steps` 是这样算的：

```yaml
num_steps: max(${wm.num_preds}, ${oc.select:loss.rollout.steps,0}) + ${wm.history_size}
```

所以：

```text
history_size=3
num_preds=1
rollout.steps=1 -> num_steps=4
rollout.steps=2 -> num_steps=5
```

即使 `rollout.weight=0`，训练 batch 的序列长度也会变。这个会影响：

```text
output["emb"] 的 T
transition_distance 看到的 pair 数量
temporal_straightness/logging
如果 regularizer.scope=full，也会影响 uniformity pair pool
```

你这份 config 里：

```yaml
regularizer.scope: pred_window
```

所以 uniformity 应该只用：

```python
reg_emb = reg_emb[:, : ctx_len + n_preds]
```

也就是前 4 帧，`rollout.steps=2` 多出来的第 5 帧不进 uniformity。这样影响小很多。

但 `transition_distance` 当前代码是对完整 `emb` 做的：

```python
dist_emb[:, :-1], dist_emb[:, 1:]
```

所以如果 `num_steps=5`，它会看 4 个 transition；如果 `num_steps=4`，它看 3 个 transition。  
不过 `detach_input=true` 时它仍然不应该改 encoder，只训练 head。

所以我会更精确地说：

```text
rollout.weight=0 -> rollout loss 不影响训练目标
rollout.steps=2 -> 仍可能改变输入序列长度，不是严格 no-op
```

但在你这份 SWM config 下，因为：

```yaml
regularizer.scope: pred_window
transition_distance.detach_input: true
```

`rollout.steps=2` 大概率不是 SWM 掉分的主因。

现在最关键的问题反而是：`transition_distance.detach_input=true` 时，理论上不该直接改变主表征。SWM 掉这么多，更需要做一个 clean sanity check：

```text
同一份 config，只把 transition_distance.weight=0
```

如果这个也低，说明不是 transition_distance 本身，而是这组 run 的其他配置/随机性。  
如果这个恢复 baseline，再说明即使 detach，新增 head/优化器/随机路径也在实际训练里造成了扰动。
````

```
set -g mouse on

# 鼠标滚轮：如果应用没有捕获鼠标，自动进入 copy-mode 滚动
bind -n WheelUpPane \
  if-shell -F "#{mouse_any_flag}" \
    "send-keys -M" \
    "if -Ft= '#{pane_in_mode}' 'send-keys -M' 'select-pane -t=; copy-mode -e; send-keys -M'"

bind -n WheelDownPane \
  select-pane -t= \; send-keys -M

# copy-mode 里鼠标滚轮速度（每次滚 3 行），同时覆盖默认和 vi 模式
bind -T copy-mode WheelUpPane      select-pane \; send-keys -X -N 3 scroll-up
bind -T copy-mode WheelDownPane    select-pane \; send-keys -X -N 3 scroll-down
bind -T copy-mode-vi WheelUpPane   select-pane \; send-keys -X -N 3 scroll-up
bind -T copy-mode-vi WheelDownPane select-pane \; send-keys -X -N 3 scroll-down

# 增大历史缓冲区
set -g history-limit 50000
```
